伏瀀愀挀椀琀礀
, Running = 
, Stamp = 
FlowBackdropMessage
Image = 
Moving = 蘀疣渀椀昀漀爀洀
 shader field;
甀渀椀昀漀爀洀
 float2 size;
 float time;
 float density;
栀愀氀昀
 hash(float2 p) {
    p = fract(p * float2(0.1031, 0.1030));
    p += dot(p, p.yx + 33.33);
    return half(fract((p.x + p.y) * p.x));
4 main(float2 p) {
    float2 uv = p / size;
    float t = time;
    float2 flow = float2(
        sin(uv.y * 3.1 + t * 0.23) + sin((uv.x + uv.y) * 2.3 - t * 0.17),
        cos(uv.x * 2.7 - t * 0.19) + cos((uv.x - uv.y) * 1.9 + t * 0.21));
    half3 c = field.eval(p + flow * 0.022 * size).rgb;
    // 压亮部：亮度超过 0.42 的部分只保留三成，浅色封面上白字也清楚；整体再略压暗
    half l = dot(c, half3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
    half knee = 0.42;
    c *= (l > knee ? (knee + (l - knee) * 0.3) / l : 1.0) * 0.86;
    // 抖动按设备像素取（运行时着色器拿不到 sk_FragCoord，用本地坐标乘显示缩放）
    c += (hash(floor(p * density)) - 0.5) / 255.0;
    return half4(c, 1.0);
